- [!extern] **P4.96** [GATE,CI] Wire the G17 JS advisory leg — `osv-scanner` over `pnpm-lock.yaml`, offline-tolerant, sharing the release-tier advisory-DB staleness floor with the Rust leg · §6.3.4 §6.7.1 · G17 G24
  > **[!extern] (L(-1)) [Co-Pilot ruling 2026-09-24 — owner may overturn]:** every deliverable is caged (`scripts/gate-tools.toml`, `scripts/check-*`, `scripts/gate-selftests/**`, `scripts/*.toml`, `lefthook.yml`, `.github/**`; G71), so the Co-Pilot lands it under owner-ack as one act, the P4.95 shape. **(1)** Pin `osv-scanner` in `gate-tools.toml` — realizability measured 2026-09-24: v2.6.0 (2026-09-14) publishes raw per-platform binaries (`asset_kind = "raw"`) for all five pinned triples, an `osv-scanner_SHA256SUMS` file and a SLSA provenance bundle (`multiple.intoto.jsonl`; corroborate with `gh attestation verify --bundle`, the per-digest attestation endpoint returns 404 for this repo). **(2)** `scripts/check-js-advisories`, the JS half of G17: `osv-scanner --offline --lockfile=pnpm-lock.yaml` against the local OSV database (`--offline` disables every network feature; `--offline-vulnerabilities` alone does not), fail-closed on any advisory outside `scripts/osv-ignores.toml` (caged by the `scripts/*.toml` glob; mirrors the Rust `audit.toml` discipline — id + reason + owner ack per entry, growth-guarded like the cargo-vet exemptions: a frozen count pinned in `check-js-advisories` itself, mirroring `check-supply-chain`'s `EXPECTED_EXEMPTION_COUNT`, so growth needs a second, reviewed edit of the caged script; an entry above it reds until the count is re-frozen under owner ack); in a local plane that never fetches, an absent database → skip WITH notice (the `cargo audit --no-fetch` posture of `check-rust-lint-contract`); at L4 the check is fail-CLOSED — an absent or partial database after the refresh step reds the job, mirroring ci.yml's fail-closed RustSec `advisory-db` warm step (gate-planes: fail-closed is the default posture, and no other plane covers this leg). **(3)** The database refresh (`--download-offline-databases`) is the separate step, cached in Lane A keyed on the calendar day with `restore-keys` falling back to the previous day's entry, so the check itself opens no socket; the download is fail-OPEN, the check after it is not — only a run where nothing restored and nothing downloaded reds. **(4)** P10.25's staleness floor reads this database's timestamp beside RustSec's; the check is wired L2 (`lefthook.yml` pre-push, beside `check-rust-lint-contract --full`) and L4 (the Lane-A `gate-tooling` job). **(5)** g24 legs run against a COMMITTED one-record fixture database (so the planted positive is never vacuous on a plane without the live database): a lock pinning `undici@7.28.0` (GHSA-4cwx-7wf7-3272, the incident) reds; the committed lock passes; an ignore entry without a reason reds and one with a reason for an advisory the scan reports passes; an ignore entry whose advisory the scan no longer reports reds as stale; an ignore file holding one entry more than its frozen count reds (the growth guard); in the fetch plane (L4, the refresh step present) an absent or partial database reds; the live-database-absent local plane skips with the notice, never silently. **(6)** Docs in the same act: the G17 row's P4.96 pointer becomes the delivered form, spec §6.7.1 step 6 names the JS half beside `cargo audit` / `cargo deny`, gate-status.md gets a gate-activation-flips log entry (G17 is always-on, not a ratchet row), the security-concept §5 row.

## The G56 transitive-action-pin half — the owner act the r6 sentence promised

> Authored by the **Co-Pilot 2026-09-24** from the sweep behind plan-lint check 33: the G56 row has promised this
> half as "its own acquisition box" since b9b01df (2026-09-02, the P4.28 tail) with no owner, and named pinact as
> its checker since the r6 review (99a7b52). Caged end to end, so `[!extern]`; max+1 convention.

- [!extern] **P4.97** [GATE,CI] Land the transitive-action-pin half of G56's r6 rule — fetch every referenced action's metadata file (`action.yml` or `action.yaml`) at its pinned SHA (fail-open, SHA-keyed cache), then a fail-closed offline check over the copies: every nested `uses:` SHA-pinned and every docker `runs.image` digest-pinned or declared-exempt, wired L2 + L4 · §6.7.1 · G56 G50 G24
  > **[!extern] (L(-1)) [Co-Pilot ruling 2026-09-24 — owner may overturn]:** every deliverable is caged (`scripts/gate-tools.toml`, `scripts/check-ci-supply-chain`, `scripts/gate-selftests/**`, `lefthook.yml`, `.github/**`; G71), the P4.96 shape. **Realizability (§11.4 (f)), measured 2026-09-24 against BOTH the tool and its inputs.** The tool: pinact v5.0.0 reads the local workflow and action files it is handed, resolves tags to SHAs and version comments through the GitHub API, and never reads a referenced REMOTE action's `action.y*ml`; its `--no-api` mode errors on a bare SHA that carries no `# <version>` comment (pinact `docs/codes/005.md`), which upstream copies commonly lack — so pinact is NOT the checker here; it stays, beside G67, an informational corroboration. The inputs: the six SHA-pinned actions the workflows reference (checkout, setup-python, cache/restore, cache/save, harden-runner, scorecard-action), fetched at their pinned SHAs — none is a composite action, none carries a nested `uses:`, and the repo has no local action, so a nested-`uses:` check alone scans nothing live today. The ONE transitive mutable reference is `ossf/scorecard-action`'s `runs.image: docker://ghcr.io/ossf/scorecard-action:v2.4.4` — a mutable tag inside a SHA-pinned `action.yaml` that G56's image-digest sub-rule (9) does not read (it covers `container:` / `services:` images, not an action's own `runs.image`). **(1)** The fetch step: for every `uses: <owner>/<repo>[/<path>]@<sha>` in `.github/workflows/**`, the action's `action.yml` OR `action.yaml` at that SHA via the GitHub contents API (scorecard-action's is `action.yaml`; a fetch that tries only `action.yml` reds every L4 run) into a cache keyed by SHA (immutable, never stale) — fail-OPEN for the download, the P4.96 (3) shape; recurse one level into nested `uses:` that are themselves SHA-pinned (the depth bound is a named residual: a tag-pinned action at depth two is not read; G67 corroborates beyond it). **(2)** The check (G56 leg (12)): over the fetched copies, every nested `uses:` carries a lowercase 40-hex SHA and every docker `runs.image` is digest-pinned (`@sha256:<64-hex>`) or listed, with a reason, in a caged exemption file (`scripts/transitive-pin-exemptions.toml`); a copy absent after the fetch step reds at L4 (fail-closed, the gate-planes default) and skips WITH notice in a local plane that never fetches. The first live run reds on scorecard-action's image tag unless the exemption is declared — the fork this act carries to the owner: exempt it with the reason (an upstream-controlled tag inside a SHA-pinned action, OSSF's own image, consumed by a scheduled read-only job) or pin the image by digest in a fork of the action. **(3)** g24 legs: a fetched-copy fixture with a tag-pinned nested `uses:` reds; a fetched-copy fixture with a tag-pinned `runs.image` reds, the same with a declared exemption passes, and with an exemption entry that lacks a reason reds; a copy absent at L4 reds; the live-tree leg derives its expectation from the workflow scan — one fetched copy per distinct remote SHA-pinned `uses:`, at least one — and asserts every copy was read and every `runs.image` classified (a monotone, data-derived pin, never an exact tally that reds on the next action a box adds). A local composite action (`uses: ./…`) is scanned in-tree, never fetched, and a `runs.image: Dockerfile` action is read at its `FROM` line under the same digest rule or a declared exemption — each arm has its own planted-positive leg in a temp-dir fixture (a tag-pinned nested `uses:` in a local action; a Dockerfile `FROM` without a digest), whatever the live tree holds: the fetched-copy fixture covers the remote arm, a depth-one fetched copy whose own nested `uses:` is tag-pinned covers the recursion arm, a plane-without-fetch fixture covers the skip-with-notice arm (the P4.96 sibling leg), the local fixtures the two in-tree arms, and the live-tree leg reads the real set. Named residual: a REMOTE action whose `runs.image` is `Dockerfile` is fetched as `action.y*ml` only, so its `FROM` line is not read — the leg records it as unclassified, never as passed. **(4)** Docs in the same act: the G56 row's (12) Delivered note and its r6 sentence, gate-status.md's activation-flips entry, and a security-concept §5 row if the exemption is a new residual class.

## The phase-end Co-Pilot hardening sweep — the standing phase-close box

> The standing test-strategy §11 phase-close box (owner directive, recorded 2026-07-06):
> Co-Pilot-executed — never the Build-Loop; mandate, level and evidence rules in
> [test-strategy §11](../process/test-strategy.md#11-the-phase-end-co-pilot-hardening-sweep).

- [!extern] **P4.81** [TEST] Run the phase-end Co-Pilot hardening sweep over the whole P4 delivery — adversarial re-test at the hardest technically-possible level · §6.4
  > **[!extern] (Co-Pilot-executed — the standing test-strategy §11 phase-close sweep, never the Build-Loop):** runs once every other P4 box is `[x]`; the phase's whole delivery is adversarially re-tested at the hardest technically-possible level with unrestricted session tooling (Docker, WebDriver/Playwright, property/fuzz/mutation probes, real-OS live runs); findings are fixed with tests as normal dual-reviewed commits before this box flips `[x]`.
  > **The P4 caged tails close here [pre-fill audit 2026-09-15]:** before this box flips `[x]`, the Co-Pilot lands, under owner-ack, every caged edit a P4 box declared as a tail that reds nothing: the compile-sanity CI invocation of the P4.52 link-kind checker (`.github/**`) with its `scripts/core-static-links.toml` allow-list, seeded from the three-OS measurement; a planted positive from outside the P4.52 link-kind checker (a caged g24 leg that runs it over a planted `static=` LGPL fixture and must fail); the planted positive from outside the tool in `g24-stage-engines.py` for every assertion arm P4.51, P4.53–P4.55 and P4.76 land (the P4.41 ruling's condition); the two P4.62 tails (the §0.7 row for `src-tauri/size-baseline.toml` with its fingerprint re-bless, and the planted positive for the `--check` baseline-binding guard); the planted positive from outside the tool in `g24-stage-engines.py` for the P4.63 lever-refusal guards; the P4.41 (7) `check-repo-invariants` leg that the committed `hash-manifest.json` keeps `"placeholder": true`; the P4.61 G72 wiring act; any `PINNED_FLOORS` row a P4 dep-add creates (`url` if P4.94 promotes it, `spdx` from P4.58); the P4.56.2 / P4.58 tool pins whose absence reds nothing.
  > **Second leg (§11.4, owner directive 2026-07-22):** the same sweep then pre-fill-audits the P5 plan boxes over every test-strategy §11.4 surface; §11.4 binds, this note only points; resolvable findings land as dual-reviewed plan/spec edits BEFORE the P5 build session starts, genuine forks go to the owner batched at the boundary.
  > **Boundary stop:** P5.1 carries `needs:` on this box — the phase-end sweep box is the one `[!extern]` that blocks its WHOLE successor phase (`_format.md` §2 / §6 step 4, test-strategy §11.3), so the loop hard-stops at the P4→P5 boundary and hands off to the Co-Pilot until the sweep is `[x]`.
